  116  def calculate_recency_weight(
  117      days_old: pd.Series,
  118      *,
  119      half_life_days: float,
  120  ) -> pd.Series:
  121      if half_life_days <= 0:
  122          raise ValueError(
  123              "half_life_days must be greater than zero."
  124          )
  125  
  126      numeric_days = pd.to_numeric(
  127          days_old,
  128          errors="coerce",
  129      )
  130  
  131      numeric_days = numeric_days.clip(
  132          lower=0,
  133      )

Lines 135-200:
  135      return np.exp(
  136          -np.log(2.0)
  137          * numeric_days
  138          / float(half_life_days)
  139      )
  140  
  141  
  142  def calculate_sample_size_weight(
  143      sample_size: pd.Series,
  144      *,
  145      reference_sample_size: float,
  146      minimum_factor: float,
  147      maximum_factor: float,
  148  ) -> tuple[pd.Series, pd.Series]:
  149      if reference_sample_size <= 0:
  150          raise ValueError(
  151              "reference_sample_size must be greater than zero."
  152          )
  153  
  154      if minimum_factor <= 0:
  155          raise ValueError(
  156              "minimum_factor must be greater than zero."
  157          )
  158  
  159      if maximum_factor < minimum_factor:
  160          raise ValueError(
  161              "maximum_factor must be at least minimum_factor."
  162          )
  163  
  164      numeric_sample = pd.to_numeric(
  165          sample_size,
  166          errors="coerce",
  167      )
  168  
  169      valid_sample = numeric_sample.where(
  170          numeric_sample > 0
  171      )
  172  
  173      imputed_sample = valid_sample.fillna(
  174          float(reference_sample_size)
  175      )
  176  
  177      factor = np.sqrt(
  178          imputed_sample
  179          / float(reference_sample_size)
  180      )
  181  
  182      factor = factor.clip(
  183          lower=float(minimum_factor),
  184          upper=float(maximum_factor),
  185      )
  186  
  187      return factor, valid_sample
  188

  200          weights,

  366          for value in row
